# Skills · V · part 72

882 entries, voltagent-design to vowi-handyverstoss-spezial. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **voltagent-design**: The findajob.pro design system (dark Voltagent theme) — colors, typography, spacing, component chrome, and do/don't rules. Use before writing or editing ANY findajob UI (templates, CSS, components, new screens), so everything matches the prototype pixel-perfectly. ([Geronimo1975/findajob-pro](https://github.com/Geronimo1975/findajob-pro/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/voltagent-design))
- **voltagent-design**: Voltagent's AI agent engineering platform design system — an unrelenting near-black canvas (#101010, no light mode) broken only by a single electric-green accent (#00d992) and hairline-bordered feature cards with no shadows. Type pairs sentence-case Inter for narrative with SF Mono for inline code, command snippets, and uppercase tracked eyebrows, giving the page a "polished documentation that also sells something" feel. Use when asked for a "Voltagent style", Voltagent-inspired UI, or a calm, hairline-bordered dark developer-tool marketing page. ([Skylarnina/Skylarnina](https://github.com/Skylarnina/Skylarnina/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/voltagent-design))
